   def project_simplex(v, b=1):
       # sort v descending
       u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
       cssv = np.cumsum(u)
       rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - b))[0][-1]

Projection target n. `project_simplex(v, n)` returns sum exactly n? due numeric maybe. It subtracts theta; if all positive. We'll implement to target `b`. Use stable:
```
def proj_simplex(v, b):
    v = np.asarray(v, float)
    n = v.size
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1)
    # threshold where u*idx > cssv-b
    rho = np.nonzero(idx * u > cssv - b)[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - b) / (rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta, 0.0)
```
Need if no index? For b>0, should. If v all nan? no.
This ensures nonnegative and sum close b if theta > min; but if v has very large negative, rho maybe 0. Good.

```
def correlation_weights(w,a):
    if FFT and a.size>1024:
       out = fftconvolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full', axes=-1)
    else:
       out = np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')
    return 2.0*out[n-1:2*n-1]
```

def gradient_optimize(a, stop, use_lp, max_iter,):
    a = normalize_to_len(a)
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); bestsc=score(a)
    step=0.1
    stale=0
    w=None; w_timer=0; lp_timer=time
    C=bthesis? we can recompute each iter.
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time>=stop: break
        C = conv(a,a); M=C.max()
        s = a.sum(); local = 2*n*M/(s*s) # s ~n
        update local best maybe
        if local < bestsc*(1-1e-14): best...
        # if stalled: try random perturbations and return if improved? We'll manage in outer.
        # choose weights
        if use weights lp changed or every 20: compute soft w
        h = corr(w,a)
        # if h too small break
        # line search:
        steps = np.geomspace(step*0.25, step*4, 8?) or fixed fractions.
        cand_best=a; cand_sc=local
        for st in [step*0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8,16?]:
            v = a - st*h
            x = project_simplex(v, n)
            if x.sum() <=0: continue
            sc = score(x)
            if sc < cand_sc: cand...
        if improved:
           a=cand; step=cand_step*(0.5-2 depending)
           stale=0
        else:
           stale +=1; step*=0.7
           if stale > 20: random perturb, maybe if improved accept; if not, break to LP phase
    return best
```

def line_search(a,h,base):
    best_x=a; best_sc=score(a); best_val=base None

- LB solve:
```
def solve_lp_weights_and_dir(a, stop):
    n=a.size; M=conv(a,a).max()
    A = make_A(a)
    # dual min sum y s.t. A.T y >= n (since sum n)
    c=np.ones(2n-1)
    A_ub=-A.T; b_ub=-n
    res=linprog(c,A_ub,b_ub,bounds=[(0,None)]*m, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    y=res.x; y=np.maximum(y,0)
    y = y/sum(y)
    # primal max sum b s.t. A b <= M
    c=-np.ones(n)
    res2=linprog(c,A,b_ub=M*ones(m),bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs',options=...)
    b=res2.x; normalize sum n
    return y,b
```

      To solve equality LS:
```
def constrained_ls(A, v):
    n=A.shape[1]
    G = A.T @ A
    G[np.diag_indices(n)] += 1e-10*np.trace(G)/n
    rhs = A.T @ v
    M = np.empty((n+1,n+1))
    M[:n,:n]=G; M[:n,-1]=1; M[-1,:n]=1; M[-1,-1]=0
    b = np.concatenate([rhs,[0]])
    sol=np.linalg.solve(M,b)
    return sol[:n]
```

Detailed code plan:

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog
import scipy.sparse as sps
try: from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except: fftconvolve=None

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
  t0=time.monotonic()
  try: budget=float(budget_s)
  except: budget=1300.0
  if budget<20: budget=20
  deadline=t0+budget-25.0  # headroom
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

  best_score=float('inf'); best_arr=None
```
  Use helper `valid_arr` returning clipped nonnegative with sum adjusted? For candidates, ensure elements finite and between 0 and 1000. Since sum target ≤n maybe for n≤5000 values ≤1000. If some values exceed 1000 due weird, clip and normalize? Since evaluator clips to 1000; local should emulate.   `fast_score(a)`:
```
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
 n=a.size
 if n==0: return inf
 s=a.sum()
 if s<0.01: return inf
 if n>0 and USE_FFT and n>1024:
    c=fftconvolve(a,a,mode='full')
    M=float(np.max(c.real))
 else:
    c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max())
 if not finite: return inf
 return 2*n*M/(s*s)
```
Using direct for n≤1024 for exactness; for large approximate okay.

```

  Interpolation:
```
def interp_len(src,n):
  if src.size==n return src.copy()
  ox=np.linspace(0,1,src.size); nx=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  out=np.interp(nx,ox,src)
  return normalize_to_sum(out,n)
```

def make_A(a, dense=None):
 n=a.size; m=2*n-1

   A=np.empty((m,n),float)

      A[k, lo:hi+1] = a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]

def corr_weights(w,a):
 n=a.size
 if fftconvolve is not None and n>2048:
   out=fftconvolve(w,a[::-1],mode='full')

   out=np.convolve(w,a[::-1],mode='full')
 return 2.0*out[n-1:2*n-1]

  Soft weights:
```
def soft_weights(C,M,rel=0.03):
 m=C.size
 if M<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
 temp=max(M*rel, M*1e-8, 1e-300)
 w=np.exp((C-M)/temp)
 # for numerical, C-M negative. Sum may under/overflow? M ~O(n), temp~0.03M, okay.
 s=w.sum()
 if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones(m)/m
 else: w/=s
 # maybe add uniform 1%
 w=0.98*w + 0.02/m
 w/=w.sum()
 return w
```

  Dual LP weights:

def solve_dual_weights(a, time_limit=5.0):
 n=a.size
 if linprog is None or n>700: return None
 A=make_A_dense(a)
 c=np.ones(2*n-1)
 A_ub=-A.T
 b_ub=-float(n)
 res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=[(0,None)]*(2*n-1),method='highs',
   options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':time_limit})
 if res is None or not res.success: return None
 y=res.x; y=np.clip(y,0,None); s=y.sum()
 if s<=0: return None
 y/=s
 return y
```

  Constrained LS flatten direction:
```
def flatten_dir(a, C, relax=0.5, ridge=1e-10):
 n=a.size; m=C.size
 if n>600: return None
 A=make_A_dense(a)
 s=a.sum(); sumC=float(C.sum())
 r=sumC/m
 t=r # target
 v=0.5*relax*(t-C)
 G=A.T@A
 tr=np.trace(G)
 if tr>0: G = G + (ridge*tr/n)*np.eye(n)
 rhs=A.T@v
 # solve KKT
 M = np.empty((n+1,n+1))
 M[:n,:n]=G; M[:n,-1]=1.0; M[-1,:n]=1.0; M[-1,-1]=0.0
 b=np.empty(n+1); b[:n]=rhs; b[-1]=0.0
 try:
    sol=np.linalg.solve(M,b)
    d=sol[:n]
 except LinAlgError:
    try:
      d, = np.linalg.lstsq(np.vstack([A, 1e6*np.ones(n)]), np.concatenate([v,0]), rcond=None)[0]
    except: return None
 if not finite: return None
 d = d - d.mean()? KKT ensures sum maybe zero but remove.
 # limit magnitude
 dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
 if dmax<1e-14: return None
 d/=dmax
 return d
```

Could improve by solving equality LS via SVD of A (2n-1 x n) maybe SVD O(n²m) similar. KKT fine.
We can compute A.T@A without storing? We have A.
Maybe use `np.linalg.lstsq(A_aug, v_aug)` with A_aug (m+1,n) `rcond=None`: singular value decomposition of 640x320, similar. KKT solve 321x321 cheaper.

Step 0.1 gives δC~0.1*0.5 diff =5%, small. Step 1 gives 50% flatten. Good. We can line search t values [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3,10?] But if dmax normalized to max1, t=10 changes by 10 (huge). def optimize(a, stop, use_lp=True, use_ls=True, grad=True, max_iters=1000000, verbose=False, lp_every=20, ls_every=10):
    n=a.size; target=sum=n
    a=normalize_to_sum(a,n)
    best=a.copy(); bestd=fast_score(a); exact_best? update_best outside
    step_g = 0.1  # max-norm step for gradient L2? 
    step_f = 0.2
    stale=0; iter=0
    w_lp=None; w_timer=time; a_L_last=None
    while time<stop:
       # maybe update best exact
       C=conv_full(a); M=C.max(); s=a.sum(); local=2*n*M/(s*s)
       if local<bestd-1e-12: best=a.copy(); bestd=local; update_best(a, force=local<best_score*0.9999?)
       # Periodic weights
       use_lp_now = use_lp and (stale>5 or iter%lp_every==0) and remaining>20 and n<=650
       if use_lp_now:
           A=make_A_dense(a) if n<2000 else None
           # dual weights
           y = solve dual using A
           if y is not None:
               w_lp = y
               # blend with soft: weights = 0.7 y +0.3 soft if y too concentrated? 
           # primal dir
           b = solve primal using A if time
           if b is not None: try line_search_mix(a,b)
       # choose weight
       if w_lp is not None and iter%lp_every < lp_every? Keep y for 10 iters? Actually y specific to a; after moves it changes. Use it for gradient direction until next LP, but maybe too stale. Soften with current C: w = 0.5*y +0.5*soft_weights(C) maybe.
       else: soft
       if grad:
          h=corr(w,a)
          g=h - h.mean(); gscale=max(np.max(abs(g)),1e-12)
          d = -g/gscale
          # maybe if scale indicates flat, perturb
          improved=t,line_search(a,d,step_g, base=0.1)
          # update step_g
       if !improved:
          stale+=1
          if stale%5==0:
             # flatten LS
             if use_ls and n<=600 and time<stop-10:
                d=flatten_dir(a,C)
                if d is not None:
                   improved,t=line_search(a,d,step_f, base=0.1)
          if stale > 30: random_perturb
       if improved: stale=0
```

Line search generic:
```
def line_search(a,d,base):
   n=a.size; target=n
   cur=fast_score(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cur; best_t=0.0
   # generate logarithmic around base
   ts = [base*f for f in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0)]
   # plus very small if base large
   ts.append(1e-4)
   # maybe ensure no >20*max? d max1, t up to 4 okay.
   for t in ts:
       v=a+t*d
       v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0) # after normalize sum? clip before norm
       s=v.sum()
       if s<1e-12: continue
       x=v*(target/s) # normalize
       # If clipping caused max>1000? reclip? For t small no.
       sc=fast_score(x)
       if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=x; best_t=t
   return best_a,best_sc,best_t
```
But if best_t at highest, we can increase base*2; if at lowest, base*=0.5. We can store. To avoid evaluating cur every time, pass current score. Line search returns improvement factor.

def apply_update(a,d,t):

The root sequence p_j ~ binom(2j,j)/4^j. For finite n, its square coefficients: first n-1 near constant, tail lower. To reduce max, we can attempt to make the square coefficients as flat as possible by adjusting tail (last few coefficients). There is a relation to one-sided prediction. Maybe we can solve finite exact using linear system for P² coefficients constant for k=0..K-1? For k < n, C_k = Σ_{i=0}^k a_i a_{k-i}. If we want the first N coefficients constant, we can recursively compute a_i? For k=0: a0²=r. k=1:2a0a1=r -> a1=r/(2a0). k=2:2a0a2 +a1²=r -> a2=(r-a1²)/(2a0), etc. This generates a sequence with initial square coefficients constant r! This is exactly solving truncation of (1-z)^-1/2? Let's check: If P² coefficients constant for k≥0 (infinite), P²=r/(1-z) -> P=√r(1-z)^-1/2, coefficients root. Finite recursion yields same first n coefficients of root until denominator? Indeed root coefficients. So initial lags constant to best possible given a0. For full finite, boundaries cause later coefficients. The root is optimal for flat left half. To flatten entire, maybe choose a finite sequence satisfying constant for both ends? We could choose P such that P² coefficients constant for first n-? and last n-? simultaneously. The root from left gives constant left. From right (reverse root) gives constant right. A sequence that interpolates/superimposes both ends? Maybe optimum is a blend of left-root and right-root? Since left-root has high left, low right; right-root (increasing) low left, high right. Symmetric blend? If average left-root and right-root, maybe central? Let's explore. Let p^L_j ∝ binom(2j,j), decreasing. Its square has C_k≈r_L for k<n, tail lower. Let p^R_j=p^L_{n-1-j} increasing (root anchored at right). Its square reversed has constant for k>n? The mixture p=(p^L+p^R)/2. What is p²? Cross term may create peaks? It may be more balanced: left and right halves elevated, maybe central? Since p^L central ~1/√n small? p^R central small; cross term maybe? Let's approximate continuous f_m = (f_left + f_right)/2 with f_left x^-1/2, f_right (1-x)^-1/2, each normalized 1/2? Sum normalized? If f=(1/2)(x^{-1/2} normalized + (1-x)^-1/2 normalized). Then f*f = 1/4(f_L*f_L + f_R*f_R +2 f_L*f_R). f_L*f_L flat 1 on [0,1] then tail; f_R*f_R flat on [1,2] then tail; cross f_L*f_R? f_L singular at0, f_R at1; their convolution maybe high at? Let's design `optimize` more concretely.

```
def optimize(a, stop, rng, check_best, use_lp, use_ls, grad, max_iters=10**9, lp_every=25, ls_every=10, base_grad=0.1, base_flatten=0.2, large=False):
    n=a.size; target=float(n)
    a=norm_to_len(a,n)
    # local best
    best_local=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    best_lp=a.copy()? not separate
    iter=0; stale=0; momentum=np.zeros(n)
    grad_base=base_grad; flat_base=base_flatten
    last_lp = last_ls = 0
    A=None; C=None
    w=None
    while time.monotonic() < stop:
        it+=1
        C=conv(a); M=C.max(); s=a.sum(); loc=2*n*M/(s*s)
        if loc < best_sc*(1-1e-13): best_sc=loc; check_best(a, force=loc<some)
        # if remaining too low break
        # LP?
        do_lp = use_lp and n<=650 and (it%lp_every==1 or stale>5) and time<stop-20
        if do_lp:
           try A=make_A(a)
           # dual
           y = solve_dual(a,A)
           if y is not None:
              # blend weights y with soft each use? Store y until next LP
              w_lp=y
           # primal
           b=solve_primal(a,A,M)
           if b is not None:
               mix=line_search_mix(a,b)
               if improved: a=mix; iter reset; stale=0; check; continue? 
        # choose weights
        Ccurr=conv(a) (maybe recompute if mixed)
        if w_lp is not None:
            w_soft = soft_weights(Ccurr,M,0.03)
            w = 0.5*w_lp + 0.5*w_soft
            w/=w.sum()
            # if after some moves, w_lp stale; every lp_every updates
        else:
            w=soft_weights(Ccurr,M)
        # gradient step
        if grad:
            h=corr(w,a)
            g=h - h.mean()
            gs=max(np.max(abs(g)),1e-12)
            d=-g/gs
            # momentum
            if MOM: v=beta*v+(1-beta)*d else v=d
            # line search v with grad_base
            a_new,sc,t = line_search(a,v,grad_base,target)
            if sc < loc*(1-1e-12):
                a=a_new; grad_base=t*(2 if t>=grad_base*0.9 else 0.6)
                stale=0; momentum=...; continue
            else:
                grad_base*=0.7; stale+=1; momentum*=? 
        # flatten after gradient fail or periodic
        if use_ls and n<=600 and (stale>=3 or it%ls_every==0) and time<stop-15:
            d=flatten_dir(a,C)
            if d is not None:
               a_new,sc,t=line_search(a,d,flat_base,target)
               if sc<loc*(1-1e-12):
                  a=a_new; flat_base=t*?; stale=0; momentum=zeros; continue
               else: flat_base*=0.7
        # random perturb if stale
        if stale > 25:
               # multiplicative perturb
               vlog=a*np.exp(0.15*rng.standard_normal(n)); a_new=norm(vlog); loc2=score
               if improved accept; stale=0
               elif stale > 80: break
    return best_local
```
Need line_search returns best and t. It may evaluate many candidates. To avoid too many exact checks, `check_best` uses local prefilter and exact.

`line_search` performance: It uses `_score` each candidate. We can cache current score to avoid recomputing; but if `a` changed via previous steps, need current. Pass current `loc`.
```
def line_search(a,d,base,target,cur_sc):
  ts = [base*0.25, base*0.5, base, base*2, base*4, base*8, 1e-4]
  # Clamp ts to positive and maybe <=10 for gradient; for flatten <=5.
  best_a=a; best_sc=cur_sc; best_t=0.0
  for t in ts:
     if t<=0: continue
     v=a+t*d
     v=np.clip(v,0,None)
     s=v.sum()
     if s<=1e-12: continue
     x=v*(target/s)
     sc=score(x)
     if sc < best_sc and sc<cur_sc:
       ...
  return x,sc,t
```

Since unit max, t=2 moves 2 units; acceptable but may zero all. Add cap: `ts = [t for t in ts if 1e-5<=t<=cap]`.
   For LP mix, d=b-a entries can be O(n) if b very different; direct t in [0,1].

Let's implement `_norm_to_len` without cap:
```
def _norm_to_len(a,target=None):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
 s=a.sum()
 if target is None: target=a.size
 if s<1e-12: a=np.ones(a.size); s=a.size
 else: a=a*(target/s)
 return a
```

Let's write `_full_conv(a)`:
```
def _full_conv(a):
  n=a.size
  if fftconvolve is not None and n > 512:
     c = fftconvolve(a, a, mode='full')
     c = np.real_if_close(c, tol=1000).real
     return c
  return np.convolve(a,a)
```

Now, code details for `_make_A` performance:
```
def _make_A(a):
    n = a.size
    m = 2*n-1
    A = np.empty((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # use local variables for speed
    for k in range(m):
        lo = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
        hi = n - 1 if k >= n else k
        A[k, lo:hi+1] = a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]
    return A
```
For k=n, k-hi-1 = n - (n-1) -1=0; slice a[n-1:0:-1] good. For k=n-1: hi=n-1, stop=n-1-(n-1)-1=-1; a[n-1:-1:-1] returns n-1..0 (but stop -1 means >-1? For negative step, stop -1 means before index len-1? Actually `list(range(5))[4:-1:-1]`? In Python, negative indices adjust: stop -1 -> 4, so start 4 stop 4 step -1 -> empty. So using stop=-1 is dangerous! Need handle negative stop separately.

```

`_score`:
```
def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return float('inf')
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float('inf')
    c = _conv_full(a)
    if c.size == 0: return inf
    M = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(M): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
```
For speed in optimize where sum target, we can use `_score_fixed(a,cur_C?)` no. OK.

def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > 700:
       c=fftconvolve(a,a,mode='full')
       c=np.real(c)
    else:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return c
```

def _corr(w,a):
    n=a.size
    ar=a[::-1]
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > 800:
        out=fftconvolve(w, ar, mode='full')
        out=np.real(out)
    else:
        out=np.convolve(w, ar)
    return 2.0*out[n-1:2*n-1]
```

`_soft_weights`:
```
def _soft_weights(C, M, relative=0.025):
    m=C.size
    if M <= 1e-300: return np.ones(m)/m
    temp = max(M*relative, M*1e-9, 1e-300)
    with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
        w = np.exp((C - M)/temp)
    # If exp underflow sum small? since one=1, sum>=1.
    s=w.sum()
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       w = np.ones(m)/m
    else:
       w = w / s
    # add small uniform
    w = 0.99*w + 0.01/m
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
C-M negative; no overflow.

`_solve_dual(a,A)`:
```
def _lp_op(c,A_ub,b_ub,bounds):
   if linprog is None: return None
   try:
      res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
   except TypeError:
      try: res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds)
      except: return None
   except: return None
   if res is None or res.x is None: return None
   if getattr(res,'success',False) or res.status==0: return res.x
   x=getattr(res,'x',None)
   if x is not None and getattr(res,'status',-1)==3? no. Return None.
```
Use `status==0` and if finite. Maybe if not success but status 2? ignore.
Bounds: `bounds=(0.0, None)` should be accepted as array? SciPy expects sequence of pairs; passing tuple of scalars maybe applies to all variables? I think `linprog` bounds can be a sequence, not tuple of scalars? It can be `[(0,None)]*n` or `scipy.optimize.Bounds(0,np.inf)`. To be safe, use list comprehension for n small. For n=1000, list of 1000 tuples okay. But for dual m up to 1200, fine. Could use `bounds=[(0.0, None)]*m`. For primal n.
```
res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, np.inf), method=...)
```

`_flatten_dir`:
```
def _flatten_dir(a,C,relax=0.6):
  n=a.size
  if n > 800: return None
  A=_make_A(a)
  sumC = float(np.sum(C))
  r = sumC / (2*n-1)
  v = 0.5 * relax * (r - C)
  G = A.T @ A
  trace = float(np.trace(G))
  if trace <= 0: return None
  reg = 1e-10 * trace / n
  if reg > 0: G = G + reg*np.eye(n)
  rhs = A.T @ v
  K = np.zeros((n+1,n+1)); K[:n,:n]=G; K[:n,-1]=1; K[-1,:n]=1; K[-1,-1]=0
  b=np.ones(n+1); b[:n]=rhs; b[-1]=0
  try: sol=np.linalg.solve(K,b)
  except: 
      # fallback with lstsq augmented
      try:
        lam=1e6
        Aa=np.vstack([A, lam*np.ones(n)])
        vv=np.concatenate([v, np.zeros(1)])
        sol=np.linalg.lstsq(Aa,vv,rcond=None)[0]; d=sol
      except: return None
      d=sol[:n]? careful variable.
```
Let's define function returns d. In try solve, sol length n+1. d=sol[:n]. In fallback, d = sol.
After d, subtract mean (should zero), finite check, normalize max to1.
```
 d -= d.mean()
 dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
 if dmax<=1e-14: return None
 return d/dmax
```
Note A.T@A may be singular heavily; ridge small. If solve fails due ill-cond, fallback.

def _line_search(a,d,base,target,cur_sc,n_cand=6,cap=2.0):

```
import numpy as np
import time, math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None
try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

_FFT_THRESHOLD = 1024
_FACTORS = (0.125,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0)

def _select_factors(n_cand):
    if n_cand <= 0: return ()
    if n_cand >= len(_FACTORS):
       f=_FACTORS
    else:
       idx = np.unique(np.linspace(0,len(_FACTORS)-1,n_cand).astype(int))
       f=tuple(_FACTORS[i] for i in idx)
    return f
```

In `_score`, call `np.asarray` and if a.size? Use raw.

`_conv_full`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
   n=a.size
   if fftconvolve is not None and n > _FFT_THRESHOLD:
      c=fftconvolve(a,a,mode='full')
      c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
      # if c has complex? np.real_if_close
   else:
      c=np.convolve(a,a)
   return c
```
If `fftconvolve` returns array length 2n-1. Good.

`_corr_weights`:
```
def _corr_weights(w,a):
   n=a.size
   if fftconvolve is not None and n > _FFT_THRESHOLD:
      out=fftconvolve(w,a[::-1],mode='full')
      out=np.asarray(out,dtype=np.float64)
   else:
      out=np.convolve(w,a[::-1])
   return 2.0 * out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If n=0 no.

`_normalize`: Use target float.
```
def _normalize(a,target=None):
 a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
 if a.size==0: return np.array([])
 a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
 s=float(a.sum())
 if target is None: target=float(a.size)
 if s<1e-12:
    return np.full(a.size, target/a.size, dtype=np.float64)
 a = a * (target/s)
 # If due roundoff, a could have nan if overflow; check
 if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s=a.sum();
    if s>0: a*= target/s
 return a
```
If a.size=0, target maybe 0; handle.

`_root`: If n<1 return ones(1). Recurrence:
```
p=np.empty(n); p[0]=1
for j in range(n-1): p[j+1]=p[j]*(2*j+1)/(2*j+2)
return _normalize(p,n)
```
For j=0 factor1/2. Good.

`_interp`:
```
def _interp(src,n):
 src=np.asarray(src,float).ravel()
 if src.size==0: return _root(n)
 if src.size==1: return _normalize(src, n)
 ox=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,src.size)
 nx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
 out=np.interp(nx,ox,src)
 return _normalize(out,n)
```

`_make_A`: as above. Use `a` ensure 1D.
```
def _make_A(a):
 n=a.size; m=2*n-1; A=np.empty((m,n),float)
 for k in range(m):
   if k < n: lo=0; hi=k
   else: lo=k-n+1; hi=n-1
   start=k-lo; stop_incl=k-hi
   if stop_incl <=0:
      seg=a[start::-1]
   else:
      seg=a[start:stop_incl-1:-1]
   A[k, lo:hi+1]=seg
 return A
```
For n=1, k=0: lo0 hi0 stop0, a[0::-1] [a0]. Good.

`_lp_solve`:
```
def _lp_solve(c,A_ub,b_ub,nvars):
 if linprog is None: return None
 try:
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*nvars
   res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
 except Exception:
   try:
      res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=(0.0,np.inf))
   except Exception:
      return None
 if res is None: return None
 x=getattr(res,'x',None)
 if x is None: return None
 x=np.asarray(x,float)
 if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
 return x
```
If HIGHS not available, `method='highs'` may error; fallback no method.

`_solve_dual`:
```
def _solve_dual(a,A):
 n=a.size; m=2*n-1
 x=_lp_solve(np.ones(m), -A.T, -float(np.ones(n))? Actually b_ub length n all -n. Use -float(n)*np.ones(n).
 return x? 
```
`b_ub = -float(n) * np.ones(n)`.

`_solve_primal`:
```
def _solve_primal(a,A,M):
 n=a.size; if M<=0: return None
 x=_lp_solve(-np.ones(n), A, M*np.ones(2*n-1), n)
 if x None return None
 b=np.clip(x,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None
 return b*(n/s)
```

`_flatten_dir` as above. Need `np.eye` maybe allocate. For n maybe 700, okay.
```
def _flatten_dir(a,C,relax=0.6):
 n=a.size
 if n < 4 or n > 800: return None
 try:
   A=_make_A(a)
   r=float(C.sum())/C.size
   v=0.5*relax*(r-C)
   G=A.T @ A
   tr=float(np.trace(G))
   if tr <= 0: return None
   reg=1e-10*tr/n
   G = G + reg*np.eye(n)
   rhs=A.T @ v
   K=np.zeros((n+1,n+1))
   K[:n,:n]=G; K[:n,-1]=1; K[-1,:n]=1
   bvec=np.empty(n+1); bvec[:n]=rhs; bvec[-1]=0
   try:
      sol=np.linalg.solve(K,bvec)
      d=sol[:n]
   except Exception:
      lam=1e5*max(1.0,float(np.max(np.abs(A))))
      Aa=np.vstack([A, lam*np.ones(n)])
      vv=np.concatenate([v, np.zeros(1)])
      try:
         d=np.linalg.lstsq(Aa,vv,rcond=None)[0]
      except Exception:
         return None
   d=np.asarray(d,float).ravel()
   if d.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): return None
   d -= d.mean()
   dm=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if dm <= 1e-14: return None
   return d/dm
 except Exception:
   return None
```
In fallback, `lam` scalar row for equality penalty; not exactly equality but okay.

`_line_search`:
```
def _line_search(a,d,base,target,cur_sc,n_cand=6,cap=2.0):
 if d is None or base <=0: return a,cur_sc,0.0
 f=_select_factors(n_cand)
 best_a=a; best_sc=cur_sc; best_t=0.0
 tried=set()
 for factor in f:
    t=base*factor
    if t <= 0 or t > cap: continue
    # if very close to previous, continue
    v=a+t*d
    # clip nonnegative
    v = np.maximum(v,0.0)
    s=v.sum()
    if s <= 1e-14: continue
    x=v*(target/s)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): continue
    sc=_score(x)
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc and sc < cur_sc:
       best_sc=sc; best_a=x; best_t=t
 return best_a,best_sc,best_t
```
Could include a tiny t if base*min(f)>1e-4; not necessary.

`_adjust`:
```
def _adjust_base(base,t,old,lo,hi):
 if t <=0: return max(lo, base*0.6)
 if t >= hi: return min(hi, base*1.5)
 if t < old*0.35: return max(lo, base*0.6)
 return base
```
But `hi` is cap; if t>=cap maybe increase.

```
def _optimize(a, stop, rng, check_best, grad=True, lp_every=30, ls_every=20, n_cand=6, base_g=0.05, base_f=0.25, max_stale=100, large=False):
    n=int(a.size); target=float(n)
    if n<4: return a
    a=_normalize(a,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    stale=0; it=0; w_lp=None; last_lp=-10**9; base_g=base_g; base_f=base_f
    lo_g=1e-5; hi_g=1.0 if not large else 0.5
    lo_f=1e-4; hi_f=1.5 if not large else 1.0
    # preselect none
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= stop: break
       it += 1
       rem = stop - now
       if rem < 3: break
       try:
          C=_conv_full(a); M=float(C.max())
       except Exception:
          break
       s=float(a.sum())
       if s <= 0: a=_normalize(_root(n),n); continue
       loc = float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
       if not math.isfinite(loc):
          a=best_a.copy(); continue
       if loc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_sc=loc; best_a=a.copy()
          check_best(a, force=True)
       # large maybe simpler
       do_lp = (linprog is not None and n <= 700 and rem > 30 and (it >= last_lp + lp_every or stale > 8) and lp_every>0)
       if do_lp:
          last_lp=it
          try:
             A=_make_A(a)
          except Exception:
             A=None
          if A is not None:
             y=_solve_dual(a,A)
             if y is not None: w_lp=y
             b=_solve_primal(a,A,M)
             if b is not None:
                best_x=a; best_mx=loc
                for fr in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0):
                   x=(1-fr)*a + fr*b
                   x=_normalize(x,n)
                   sc=_score(x)
                   if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_mx:
                      best_mx=sc; best_x=x
                if best_x is not a and best_mx < loc*(1-1e-12):
                   a=best_x; stale=0
                   if best_mx < best_sc*(1-1e-12):
                      best_sc=best_mx; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a,force=True)
                   else:
                      check_best(a)
                   continue
       # weights
       if w_lp is not None and (it - last_lp) < max(1, lp_every//2 if lp_every>0 else 0):
           ws=_soft_weights(C,M,0.03)
           w=0.5*w_lp + 0.5*ws
           w=sum?; w_lp remains for next? Actually we want use y for few iters.
       else:
           w=_soft_weights(C,M,0.03)
       # gradient
       if grad:
          try:
             h=_corr_weights(w,a)
             g=h - h.mean()
             gs=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
          except Exception:
             gs=0.0
          if gs > 1e-13:
             d=-g/gs
             x,sc,t = _line_search(a,d,base_g,target,loc,n_cand= n_cand, cap=hi_g)
             if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1-1e-12):
                a=x; base_g=_adjust_base(base_g,t,base_g,lo_g,hi_g); stale=0
                if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12):
                   best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a,force=True)
                else: check_best(a)
                continue
          stale += 1
          base_g=max(lo_g, base_g*0.75)
       # flatten
       if ls_every>0 and n<=800 and rem>20 and (stale > 3 or it >= last_ls + ls_every):
          last_ls=it
          d=_flatten_dir(a,C,relax=0.7)
          if d is not None:
             x,sc,t=_line_search(a,d,base_f,target,loc,n_cand=max(4,n_cand-2), cap=hi_f)
             if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1-1e-12):
                a=x; base_f=_adjust_base(base_f,t,base_f,lo_f,hi_f); stale=0
                if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12): best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a,force=True)
                else: check_best(a)
                continue
             else:
                stale += 1; base_f=max(lo_f, base_f*0.7)
       # random restart
       if stale > 25:
          improved=False
          for strength in (0.08,0.2,0.4):
             if time.monotonic() >= stop - 2: break
             try:
                v=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
                v=_normalize(v,n)
                sc=_score(v)
             except Exception:
                sc=float('inf')
             if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1-1e-12):
                a=v; sc=loc; stale=0; improved=True
                if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12): best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a,force=True)
                else: check_best(a)
                break
          if not improved:
             # try interp root blend
             root=_root(n)
             v=_normalize((1-0.5)*a + 0.5*root,n)
             sc=_score(v)
             if sc < loc: accept...
          if not improved:
             if stale > max_stale:
                break
          else:
             continue
       if stale > max_stale: break
    return best_a
```

def check_best(a, force=False):
 nonlocal best_score,best_arr,last_exact
 try:
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 except: return
 if arr.size==0: return
 arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000.0)
 if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
 if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01: return
 fast=_score(arr)
 if not math.isfinite(fast): return
 now=time.monotonic()
 if not force:
    if fast > best_score + 1e-8: return
    if fast > best_score - 1e-9 and now - last_exact < 1.0: return
 try:
    v=eval_fn([float(x) for x in arr])
 except Exception:
    return
 if math.isfinite(v) and v < best_score - 1e-14:
    best_score=v; best_arr=arr.copy(); last_exact=now
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None
try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

_FFT_THRESHOLD = 1024
_FACTORS = (0.125,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0)

def _select_factors(n_cand):
    if n_cand <= 0:
        return ()
    if n_cand >= len(_FACTORS):
       return _FACTORS
    idx = np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(_FACTORS)-1, int(n_cand)).astype(int))
    return tuple(_FACTORS[int(i)] for i in idx)
```
If n_cand not int? We'll cast in calls.

`_score`:
```
def _score(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if arr.size == 0: return float('inf')
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return float('inf')
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float('inf')
    c = _conv_full(arr)
    if c.size == 0: return float('inf')
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(M): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
```

`_conv_full` with direct for n <= threshold. Need ensure `c` is float64. `np.convolve` returns float. `fftconvolve` if not available.

`_normalize` if a.size=0 return empty. If target float.

`_root`: for n=0? return empty. But propose uses n>0. If n=0, empty.

`_make_A` uses `a` contiguous. If a.size=0, m=-1? not called.

`_lp_solve`:
```
def _lp_solve(c, A_ub, b_ub, nvars):
    if linprog is None or nvars <=0: return None
    c = np.asarray(c, float)
    A_ub = np.asarray(A_ub, float)
    b_ub = np.asarray(b_ub, float)
    bounds = (0.0, np.inf)
    last_err = None
    # try highs with presolve
    for use_highs, opts in [(True, {'presolve': True}), (True, None), (False, None)]:
        try:
            if use_highs:
                if opts is not None:
                    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
                else:
                    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
            else:
                res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)
            x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
            if x is None:
                continue
            x = np.asarray(x, float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                continue
            return x
        except Exception as e:
            last_err = e
            continue
    return None
```
If HIGHS solves but not optimal, still returns x. Could return nonoptimal; but line search improves. Fine. If status unbounded? x may inf; filter. If infeasible x None? continue.

Could a nonoptimal x from presolve fail? okay.

`_solve_dual`, `_solve_primal`:
```
def _solve_dual(a,A):
 n=a.size; if n==0 return None
 x=_lp_solve(np.ones(2*n-1), -A.T, -float(n)*np.ones(n), 2*n-1)
 if x is None: return None
 y=np.clip(x,0,None); s=y.sum()
 if s <= 1e-12: return None
 return y/s
```
If A.T negative? no.

`_solve_primal`:
```
def _solve_primal(a,A,M):
 if M <= 0: return None
 n=a.size
 x=_lp_solve(-np.ones(n), A, M*np.ones(2*n-1), n)
 if x is None: return None
 b=np.clip(x,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12 return None
 return b*(n/s)
```

   t=min(base, cap*0.5)
   if t>0: ts=[t]
   else: return a,cur_sc,0.0
# Ensure a very small t included

`_optimize` code details:
```
def _optimize(a, stop, rng, check_best, grad=True, lp_every=30, ls_every=20, n_cand=6, base_g=0.05, base_f=0.25, max_stale=100):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n < 4:
       return a
    target=float(n)
    a=_normalize(a,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    stale=0; it=0; w_lp=None; last_lp=-10**9; last_ls=-10**9
    base_g=float(base_g); base_f=float(base_f)
    lo_g=1e-5; hi_g=1.2
    lo_f=1e-4; hi_f=1.5
    while time.monotonic() < stop:
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=stop-now
       if rem < 4: break
       it += 1
       moved=False
       try:
          C=_conv_full(a)
          M=float(np.max(C))
       except Exception:
          break
       s=float(a.sum())
       if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s):
          a=best_a.copy(); continue
       loc=float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
       if not math.isfinite(loc):
          a=best_a.copy(); continue
       if loc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_sc=loc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
       # LP
       if lp_every > 0 and linprog is not None and n <= 700 and rem > 35 and (it >= last_lp + lp_every or stale > 10):
           last_lp=it
           try: A=_make_A(a)
           except Exception: A=None
           if A is not None:
              y=_solve_dual(a,A)
              if y is not None: w_lp=y
              b=_solve_primal(a,A,M)
              if b is not None:
                 mix_x=None; mix_sc=loc
                 for fr in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0):
                    x=(1.0-fr)*a + fr*b
                    x=_normalize(x,n)
                    sc=_score(x)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < mix_sc:
                       mix_sc=sc; mix_x=x
                 if mix_x is not None and mix_sc < loc*(1.0-1e-12):
                    a=mix_x
                    if mix_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                       best_sc=mix_sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
                    moved=True; stale=0
       if not moved:
          # gradient
          if grad:
             ws=_soft_weights(C,M,0.03)
             if w_lp is not None and (it - last_lp) <= max(1, lp_every//3 if lp_every>0 else 0):
                w=0.5*w_lp + 0.5*ws
                ws2=float(w.sum())
                if ws2>0: w=w/ws2
                else: w=ws
             else:
                w=ws
             try:
                h=_corr_weights(w,a)
                g=h - float(np.mean(h))
                gs=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
             except Exception:
                gs=0.0
             if gs > 1e-13:
                d=-g/gs
                x,sc,t=_line_search(a,d,base_g,target,loc,n_cand=int(n_cand),cap=hi_g)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1.0-1e-12):
                   a=x
                   # adjust base
                   if t >= hi_g:
                       base_g=hi_g
                   elif t < base_g*0.35:
                       base_g=max(lo_g, base_g*0.65)
                   else:
                       base_g=min(hi_g, base_g*1.15)
                   if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                       best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
                   moved=True; stale=0
             if not moved:
                base_g=max(lo_g, base_g*0.85)
          # flatten
          if not moved and ls_every > 0 and n <= 800 and rem > 25 and (stale > 3 or it >= last_ls + ls_every):
              last_ls=it
              d=_flatten_dir(a,C,relax=0.7)
              if d is not None:
                 x,sc,t=_line_search(a,d,base_f,target,loc,n_cand=max(4,int(n_cand)-2),cap=hi_f)
                 if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1.0-1e-12):
                    a=x
                    if t >= hi_f: base_f=hi_f
                    elif t < base_f*0.35: base_f=max(lo_f, base_f*0.65)
                    else: base_f=min(hi_f, base_f*1.15)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                       best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
                    moved=True; stale=0
                 else:
                    base_f=max(lo_f, base_f*0.8)
       if not moved:
          stale += 1
          if stale > 25 and rng is not None:
             # random multiplicative
             for strength in (0.08,0.2,0.4):
                if time.monotonic() >= stop - 2: break
                try:
                   v=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
                   v=_normalize(v,n)
                   sc=_score(v)
                except Exception:
                   sc=float('inf')
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc*(1.0-1e-12):
                   a=v
                   if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                       best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
                   moved=True; stale=0
                   break
          if not moved and stale > 35:
             # blend with root
             try:
                rt=_root(n)
                for mixw in (0.2,0.4,0.6):
                   v=(1-mixw)*a + mixw*rt
                   v=_normalize(v,n)
                   sc=_score(v)
                   if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc:
                       a=v
                       if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); check_best(a)
                       stale=0
                       break
             except Exception:
                pass
             if not moved and stale > max_stale:
                break
       # if moved, continue; else loop
    check_best(best_a, force=True)
    return best_a
```
In gradient, if gs=0, not moved, base reduce? We do `if not moved: base_g=max...` after attempt? In code above, `if not moved: base_g...` after gradient if no move, but then flatten could move; okay. But if gs=0, skip, base reduces. Fine.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

_FFT_THRESHOLD = 1024
_FACTORS = (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)


def _select_factors(n_cand):
    n_cand = int(max(0, n_cand))
    if n_cand <= 0:
        return ()
    if n_cand >= len(_FACTORS):
        return _FACTORS
    idx = np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(_FACTORS) - 1, n_cand).astype(int))
    return tuple(_FACTORS[int(i)] for i in idx)


def _conv_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > _FFT_THRESHOLD:
        c = fftconvolve(a, a, mode="full")
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    return c


def _score(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _normalize(a, target=None):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if target is None:
        target = float(arr.size)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(arr.shape, float(target) / float(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr * (float(target) / s)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if s2 > 1e-15 and abs(s2 - float(target)) > 1e-9:
        arr = arr * (float(target) / s2)
    return arr


def _root_sequence(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for j in range(n - 1):
        p[j + 1] = p[j] * (2.0 * j + 1.0) / (2.0 * j + 2.0)
    return _normalize(p, n)


def _interp_length(src, n):
    n = int(n)
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == 0:
        return _root_sequence(n)
    if src.size == 1:
        return _normalize(np.full(n, float(src[0])), n)
    ox = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size)
    nx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(nx, ox, np.clip(src, 0.0, None))
    return _normalize(out, n)


def _make_A(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.empty((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(m):
        if k < n:
            lo = 0
            hi = k
        else:
            lo = k - n + 1
            hi = n - 1
        start = k - lo
        stop_incl = k - hi
        if stop_incl <= 0:
            seg = a[start::-1]
        else:
            seg = a[start:stop_incl - 1:-1]
        A[k, lo:hi + 1] = seg
    return A


def _lp_solve(c, A_ub, b_ub, nvars):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    A_ub = np.asarray(A_ub, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.asarray(b_ub, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    bounds = (0.0, np.inf)
    attempts = (
        {"bound": "highs", "opts": {"presolve": True}},
        {"bound": "highs", "opts": None},
        {"bound": None, "opts": None},
    )
    for att in attempts:
        try:
            if att["opts"] is not None:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method=(att["bound"] or "highs"),
                    options=att["opts"],
                )
            else:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method=(att["bound"] or "highs"),
                )
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is None:
                continue
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if x.size != int(nvars) or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                continue
            return x
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def _solve_dual_weights(a, A):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or A is None:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    x = _lp_solve(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64), -A.T, -float(n) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), m)
    if x is None:
        return None
    y = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return y / s


def _solve_primal_direction(a, A, M):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or A is None or M <= 0:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    x = _lp_solve(-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), A, float(M) * np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64), n)
    if x is None:
        return None
    b = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return b * (float(n) / s)


def _flatten_direction(a, C, relax=0.7):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 4 or n > 800 or C.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return None
    try:
        A = _make_A(a)
        r = float(C.sum()) / float(C.size)
        v = 0.5 * float(relax) * (r - C)
        G = A.T @ A
        tr = float(np.trace(G))
        if tr <= 0:
            return None
        G = G + (1e-10 * tr / float(n)) * np.eye(n, dtype=np.float64)
        rhs = A.T @ v
        K = np.zeros((n + 1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        K[:n, :n] = G
        K[:n, -1] = 1.0
        K[-1, :n] = 1.0
        bvec = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        bvec[:n] = rhs
        bvec[-1] = 0.0
        try:
            sol = np.linalg.solve(K, bvec)
            d = sol[:n]
        except Exception:
            lam = 1e5 * max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(A))))
            Aa = np.vstack([A, lam * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)])
            vv = np.concatenate([v, np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)])
            d = np.linalg.lstsq(Aa, vv, rcond=None)[0]
        d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if d.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            return None
        d -= float(d.mean())
        dm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if dm <= 1e-14:
            return None
        return d / dm
    except Exception:
        return None


def _corr_weights(w, a):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > _FFT_THRESHOLD:
        out = fftconvolve(w, a[::-1], mode="full")
        out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = np.convolve(w, a[::-1])
    return 2.0 * out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _soft_weights(C, M, rel=0.03):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = C.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0)
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    temp = max(float(M) * float(rel), float(M) * 1e-9, 1e-300)
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        w = np.exp((C - float(M)) / temp)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        w = w / s
    w = 0.99 * w + 0.01 / float(m)
    s2 = float(w.sum())
    if s2 > 0 and math.isfinite(s2):
        w = w / s2
    return w


def _line_search(a, d, base, target, cur_sc, n_cand=6, cap=2.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    base = float(base)
    cap = float(cap)
    if d.size != a.size or base <= 0 or cap <= 0:
        return a.copy(), float(cur_sc), 0.0

    factors = _select_factors(n_cand)
    ts = []
    for f in factors:
        t = base * float(f)
        if t > 0 and t <= cap:
            ts.append(t)
    if not ts:
        t = min(base, 0.5 * cap)
        if t > 0:
            ts.append(t)
        else:
            return a.copy(), float(cur_sc), 0.0
    if ts[0] > 1e-5:
        ts.append(min(1e-5, 0.5 * cap))
    ts = sorted(set(ts))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(cur_sc)
    best_t = 0.0
    for t in ts:
        v = a + t * d
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            continue
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = float(v.sum())
        if s <= 1e-14:
            continue
        x = v * (float(target) / s)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            continue
        sc = _score(x)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc and sc < float(cur_sc):
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = x
            best_t = t
    return best_a, best_sc, best_t


def _optimize(a, stop, rng, check_best,
              grad=True, lp_every=30, ls_every=20, n_cand=6,
              base_g=0.05, base_f=0.25, max_stale=100):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 4:
        return a
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    target = float(n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        a = _root_sequence(n)
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = _score(a)

    stale = 0
    it = 0
    w_lp = None
    last_lp = -10**9
    last_ls = -10**9
    base_g = float(base_g)
    base_f = float(base_f)
    lo_g = 1e-5
    hi_g = 1.2
    lo_f = 1e-4
    hi_f = 1.5

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stop:
            break
        rem = stop - now
        if rem < 4:
            break
        it += 1
        moved = False

        try:
            C = _conv_full(a)
            M = float(np.max(C))
        except Exception:
            break
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            a = best_a.copy()
            continue
        loc = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))
        if not math.isfinite(loc):
            a = best_a.copy()
            continue

        if loc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = loc
            best_a = a.copy()
            check_best(a)

        if (
            lp_every > 0
            and linprog is not None
            and n <= 700
            and rem > 35
            and (it >= last_lp + lp_every or stale > 10)
        ):
            last_lp = it
            try:
                A = _make_A(a)
            except Exception:
                A = None
            if A is not None:
                y = _solve_dual_weights(a, A)
                if y is not None:
                    w_lp = y
                bdir = _solve_primal_direction(a, A, M)
                if bdir is not None:
                    mix_x = None
                    mix_sc = loc
                    for fr in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.0):
                        x = (1.0 - fr) * a + fr * bdir
                        x = _normalize(x, n)
                        sc = _score(x)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < mix_sc:
                            mix_sc = sc
                            mix_x = x
                    if mix_x is not None and mix_sc < loc * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                        a = mix_x
                        if mix_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                            best_sc = mix_sc
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            check_best(a)
                        moved = True
                        stale = 0

        if not moved and grad:
            ws = _soft_weights(C, M, 0.03)
            if w_lp is not None and lp_every > 0 and (it - last_lp) <= max(1, lp_every // 3):
                w = 0.5 * w_lp + 0.5 * ws
                wsu = float(w.sum())
                if wsu > 0 and math.isfinite(wsu):
                    w = w / wsu
                else:
                    w = ws
            else:
                w = ws

            try:
                h = _corr_weights(w, a)
                g = h - float(np.mean(h))
                gs = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            except Exception:
                gs = 0.0

            if gs > 1e-13:
                d = -g / gs
                x, sc, t = _line_search(a, d, base_g, target, loc, int(n_cand), hi_g)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    a = x
                    if t >= hi_g:
                        base_g = hi_g
                    elif t < base_g * 0.35:
                        base_g = max(lo_g, base_g * 0.65)
                    else:
                        base_g = min(hi_g, base_g * 1.15)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        check_best(a)
                    moved = True
                    stale = 0

            if not moved:
                base_g = max(lo_g, base_g * 0.85)

        if (
            not moved
            and ls_every > 0
            and n <= 800
            and rem > 25
            and (stale > 3 or it >= last_ls + ls_every)
        ):
            last_ls = it
            d = _flatten_direction(a, C, relax=0.7)
            if d is not None:
                x, sc, t = _line_search(a, d, base_f, target, loc, max(4, int(n_cand) - 2), hi_f)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    a = x
                    if t >= hi_f:
                        base_f = hi_f
                    elif t < base_f * 0.35:
                        base_f = max(lo_f, base_f * 0.65)
                    else:
                        base_f = min(hi_f, base_f * 1.15)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        check_best(a)
                    moved = True
                    stale = 0
                else:
                    base_f = max(lo_f, base_f * 0.8)

        if not moved:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 25 and rng is not None:
                for strength in (0.08, 0.20, 0.40):
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop - 2:
                        break
                    try:
                        v = a * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
                        v = _normalize(v, n)
                        sc = _score(v)
                    except Exception:
                        sc = float("inf")
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                        a = v
                        if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                            best_sc = sc
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            check_best(a)
                        moved = True
                        stale = 0
                        break
            if not moved and stale > 35:
                try:
                    rt = _root_sequence(n)
                    for mixw in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
                        v = (1.0 - mixw) * a + mixw * rt
                        v = _normalize(v, n)
                        sc = _score(v)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc:
                            a = v
                            if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                                best_sc = sc
                                best_a = a.copy()
                                check_best(a)
                            moved = True
                            stale = 0
                            break
                except Exception:
                    pass
                if not moved and stale > int(max_stale):
                    break

    check_best(best_a, force=True)
    return best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    headroom = min(35.0, max(10.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - headroom

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eval_fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None
    last_exact = 0.0

    def check_best(a, force=False):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr, last_exact
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            return
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
            return
        fast = _score(arr)
        if not math.isfinite(fast):
            return
        now = time.monotonic()
        if not force:
            if fast > best_score + 1e-8:
                return
            if fast > best_score - 1e-9 and (now - last_exact) < 1.0:
                return
        try:
            if eval_fn is not None:
                v = eval_fn([float(x) for x in arr])
            else:
                v = fast
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(v) and v < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = float(v)
            best_arr = arr.copy()
            last_exact = now

    parent = None
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                parent = _normalize(p, p.size)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n0 = int(parent.size)
        check_best(parent, force=True)
    else:
        n0 = 320

    if best_arr is None:
        r0 = _root_sequence(n0)
        check_best(r0, force=True)
    else:
        r0 = _root_sequence(n0)
        check_best(r0)

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _root_sequence(max(16, n0))
        check_best(best_arr, force=True)

    # Main intensive pass at the parent/native length.
    if parent is not None:
        main_stop = t0 + max(60.0, 0.55 * budget)
        main_stop = min(deadline - 25.0, main_stop)
        if main_stop < t0 + 30.0:
            main_stop = max(t0 + 20.0, deadline - 10.0)
        if time.monotonic() < main_stop - 10.0:
            a = _optimize(
                parent.copy(),
                main_stop,
                rng,
                check_best,
                grad=True,
                lp_every=30,
                ls_every=20,
                n_cand=7,
                base_g=0.05,
                base_f=0.25,
                max_stale=130,
            )
            check_best(a, force=True)

    # A comparable native-length pure root restart.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        a = _root_sequence(n0)
        stop = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + max(80.0, 0.12 * budget))
        a = _optimize(
            a,
            stop,
            rng,
            check_best,
            grad=True,
            lp_every=45,
            ls_every=25,
            n_cand=7,
            base_g=0.08,
            base_f=0.25,
            max_stale=90,
        )
        check_best(a, force=True)

    # Explore nearby and larger lengths.
    base_len = int(best_arr.size) if best_arr is not None else n0
    preferred = [
        base_len + 32,
        base_len + 64,
        base_len + 96,
        base_len + 128,
        512,
        768,
        1024,
        1536,
        base_len - 32,
    ]
    tried = {int(best_arr.size) if best_arr is not None else -1}
    lengths = []
    for L in preferred:
        L = int(L)
        if 16 <= L <= 2048 and L not in tried:
            lengths.append(L)
            tried.add(L)
    lengths = lengths[:7]

    for idx, L in enumerate(lengths):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 40.0:
            break
        is_last = (idx == len(lengths) - 1)
        cap_alloc = 180.0
        alloc = min(cap_alloc, rem * 0.70) if not is_last else max(30.0, rem - 30.0)
        alloc = min(alloc, max(18.0, rem * 0.9))
        if alloc < 18.0:
            break
        stop = time.monotonic() + alloc

        if L <= 800:
            if best_arr is not None and best_arr.size > 4:
                init = _interp_length(best_arr, L)
            else:
                init = _root_sequence(L)
            a = _optimize(
                init,
                stop,
                rng,
                check_best,
                grad=True,
                lp_every=30 if L <= 700 else 0,
                ls_every=20 if L <= 550 else 0,
                n_cand=5,
                base_g=0.05,
                base_f=0.20,
                max_stale=80,
            )
            check_best(a, force=True)

            rem2 = stop - time.monotonic()
            if rem2 > 30.0 and L <= 700:
                ar = _root_sequence(L)
                ar = _optimize(
                    ar,
                    time.monotonic() + min(70.0, rem2 * 0.60),
                    rng,
                    check_best,
                    grad=True,
                    lp_every=50,
                    ls_every=0,
                    n_cand=5,
                    base_g=0.07,
                    base_f=0.20,
                    max_stale=70,
                )
                check_best(ar, force=True)
        else:
            ar = _root_sequence(L)
            ar = _optimize(
                ar,
                stop,
                rng,
                check_best,
                grad=True,
                lp_every=0,
                ls_every=0,
                n_cand=4,
                base_g=0.045,
                base_f=0.20,
                max_stale=70,
            )
            check_best(ar, force=True)

            rem2 = stop - time.monotonic()
            if rem2 > 30.0 and best_arr is not None and best_arr.size > 8:
                a2 = _interp_length(best_arr, L)
                a2 = _optimize(
                    a2,
                    time.monotonic() + min(50.0, rem2 * 0.60),
                    rng,
                    check_best,
                    grad=True,
                    lp_every=0,
                    ls_every=0,
                    n_cand=4,
                    base_g=0.045,
                    base_f=0.20,
                    max_stale=60,
                )
                check_best(a2, force=True)

    # Final exact validation and safe fallback.
    if best_arr is None or best_arr.size == 0:
        best_arr = np.ones(96, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(final)):
        final = np.nan_to_num(final, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(final.sum()) < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(96, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        if eval_fn is not None:
            vf = eval_fn([float(x) for x in final])
        else:
            vf = _score(final)
        if not math.isfinite(vf):
            final = np.ones(96, dtype=np.float64)
            if eval_fn is not None:
                vf = eval_fn([float(x) for x in final])
            else:
                vf = _score(final)
    except Exception:
        final = np.ones(96, dtype=np.float64)

    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(96, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
